artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:10:This strategy narrows the AMB-668 bucket/object layout into released-pack path, version, and supersession rules. It applies only to public, non-user-specific Source Atlas artifacts.
artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:12:It does not publish packs, create credentials, provision buckets, implement release tooling, implement runtime fetch, mark packs runtime-eligible, or perform live R2 writes.
artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:16:Every released pack artifact is addressed by a content-bound path. Once a released object exists at a path, its body and integrity metadata are never mutated in place.
artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:21:<ring>/<family>/<domain>/<schema>/<artifact-id>/<version>/<sha256>/<filename>
artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:32:- `ring` is the release ring: `dev`, `staging`, or `prod`.
artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:34:- `domain` is a public Source Atlas domain slug, or `global` when the artifact is cross-domain.
artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:41:No segment may contain user text, user identifiers, private goals, private locations, device identifiers, account ids, token material, or raw source-needed text.
artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:45:Versions identify release intent. Hashes identify exact bytes. Runtime and tooling must require both.

artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:51:| Revision suffix | `v1.2.0-r2` | Repair release when the release receipt records the prior bad version and rollback/supersession reason. |
artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:53:Version-only lookup is never enough for runtime eligibility. A current manifest must point to the exact path, expected hash, signature/checksum state, schema, freshness, revocation state, compatibility envelope, release receipt, and rollback target.
artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:67:- supersession reason class: `source_changed`, `freshness_expired`, `security_revoked`, `schema_replaced`, `policy_updated`, `data_error`, or `rollback`

artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:72:Old paths remain addressable for audit, rollback, and receipt verification unless a future revocation manifest blocks them. Addressable does not mean runtime-eligible.
artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:90:- `freshness`

artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:99:Immutable pack objects are long-cache candidates because their path includes version and hash. Manifest, freshness, revocation, compatibility, and rollback objects are short-cache candidates because they encode current selection and safety state.
artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:103:- Hash-addressed pack paths can use long TTLs after future compatibility proof.
artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:104:- Current manifests, revocation lists, freshness manifests, compatibility tables, and rollback manifests use short TTLs.
artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:107:- Quarantined or revoked paths are retained only for local audit/receipt behavior when safe and must not drive current recommendations.
artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:113:- released pack body changes without path change
artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:114:- version changes without release receipt
artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:116:- supersession without reason, effective date, replacement target, and release receipt
artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:117:- runtime eligibility from a pack that lacks source binding, freshness, revocation, release receipt, rollback, and compatibility proof
artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:118:- private user data in any path, object body, metadata, manifest, validation report, release receipt, request, log, screenshot, or support artifact
artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:122:If a released pack is bad, the repair path is:
artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:124:1. Publish a revocation or rollback manifest that names the bad artifact id, version, and sha256.
artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:126:3. Publish a new current manifest that points to the replacement or last-known-good path.
artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:128:5. Record the release receipt and rollback reason.
artifacts/source-atlas-factory/r2/R2_IMMUTABLE_PACK_PATH_STRATEGY.md:134:This artifact does not implement release tooling, publish packs, configure Cloudflare/R2, create buckets, create credentials, perform live R2 writes, validate live R2 operations, implement runtime fetch/cache/quarantine behavior, mark packs runtime-eligible, approve privacy/legal posture, prove release readiness, prove accessibility, prove device behavior, or prove performance.

docs/truth/PRODUCT_DESIGN_TRUTH.md:206:Ambitions is an object-first native iPhone product, not a page-first productivity app.
docs/truth/PRODUCT_DESIGN_TRUTH.md:213:Ambitions is a market-defining, premium native iPhone-first, local-first Personal Life Organization and Execution Operating System. It subsumes and improves the jobs of task apps, calendar/planning apps, habit trackers, productivity apps, goal planners, and AI planning tools. It is all of these made personal through a local-first runtime that crafts goal plans, recommended steps, time-fit execution, proof, recovery, and reflow uniquely for each end user.
docs/truth/PRODUCT_DESIGN_TRUTH.md:215:Ambitions may contain tasks, calendar replacement behavior, habits, productivity workflows, and AI/runtime intelligence, but it must not collapse into the commodity UI or architecture patterns of those categories: generic to-do list, calendar clone, streak/shame habit tracker, dashboard stack, chatbot wrapper, web-app shell, or cloud-LLM-first planner.
docs/truth/PRODUCT_DESIGN_TRUTH.md:219:Ambitions is broader than a long-term goal executor. Long-term goals are one major layer, but the product also manages daily reality, commitments, ideas, routines, pivots, recovery, proof, planning defaults, personal growth, and changing constraints.

docs/truth/PRODUCT_UPGRADES_VISION.md:3:This document details three sovereign product upgrades designed to elevate the native iPhone-first experience of **Ambitions**. These upgrades strictly adhere to the core moats, local-first privacy boundaries, and elite Apple quiet luxury design parameters defined in the [Active Product Design Truth](file:///c:/Users/Devan/Documents/GitHub/ambitions/docs/truth/PRODUCT_DESIGN_TRUTH.md).
docs/truth/PRODUCT_UPGRADES_VISION.md:45:*   **Zero Cloud Dependence**: All visual rendering, transitions, and timing calculations occur 100% locally on-device.
docs/truth/PRODUCT_UPGRADES_VISION.md:55:  |---> Full Swipe Right  -> Complete (Saves full receipt)
docs/truth/PRODUCT_UPGRADES_VISION.md:64:2.  **Quiet Reflow Engine**: The system calculates the remaining fraction of the task, checks tomorrow's [LifeShape Field](file:///c:/Users/Devan/Documents/GitHub/ambitions/docs/truth/PRODUCT_DESIGN_TRUTH.md#L119) capacity, and quietly schedules the remainder without triggering alerts or overdue markers.
docs/truth/PRODUCT_UPGRADES_VISION.md:75:Capture interfaces in generic notes apps feel clinical and static. The **Atmosphere Composer** transforms capture into a satisfying, tactile, on-device sensory ritual that removes all friction from externalizing thought.
docs/truth/PRODUCT_UPGRADES_VISION.md:85:1.  **Dynamic Tactile Weight**: As the user types into the composer, the keystroke haptics dynamically adjust. Short sentences feel feather-light; as the thought gains length, the typing haptics take on subtle "weight" and resistance, giving the user's thought a physical on-device presence.

docs/codex/ambitions-ui-primitives-inventory.md:10:- **Duplication Risks**: Medium. docs/codex/ambitions-ui-primitives-inventory.md:11:- **Biggest Gaps**: No glaring gaps were identified in standard SwiftUI views. docs/codex/ambitions-ui-primitives-inventory.md:32:- **CelestialField**: A gravity-drift spatial micro-particle background reacting to device tilt. Includes a `Canvas` representation for particles.
docs/codex/ambitions-ui-primitives-inventory.md:66:- **Safe Consolidation Plan**: If particles are needed elsewhere, abstract the `Canvas` rendering loop inside `AtmosphereComposerCanvas` into a reusable `CelestialParticleEngine` within `AmbitionsPremiumMaterials.swift`. Do not duplicate the logic.
